Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on structured reasoning tasks, but what they encode and whether it informs model behavior remain unclear. We investigate this question through geometric reasoning, using parametric CAD constraints as a controlled testbed for separating local pairwise relations from sketch-level constraint status. By probing the hidden states of six frozen decoder-only LLMs, we examine four properties: linear decodability, forced-choice generation, activation-level influence, and behavioral steerability. Pretraining substantially improves the decoding of local geometric relations, and this advantage persists after accounting for positional cues with shuffled-order controls. In contrast, sketch-level DOF status is already highly decodable from randomly initialized representations and improves only modestly with pretraining, indicating that much of its probe performance is available without learned weights. Further analyses show that decodable information is not always actionable. Generation often fails to express this information, and on the two intervention-tested backbones, activation-restoration effects at the patched entity position vanish while decodability persists across depth. Mean-difference steering also does not reliably control outputs. These results show that decodability, generation, activation-level influence, and steerability can diverge in the tested setting. The audit provides a controlled way to distinguish failures to encode geometric structure from failures to express or control encoded information.

</details>


### [126] [BayesPrompt: human readable prompts that make sense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17866)
*Franky Kevin Nando Tezoh,Ali Hussaini Umar,Alessandro Laio,Guido Sanguinetti,Riccardo Rende*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM提示词优化问题重新定义为贝叶斯后验推断，通过高效采样算法获取既高效（低困惑度）又人类可读的提示词，在真实数据集上相较于现有方法在多项指标上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词优化方法通过最小化目标答案的困惑度来生成提示词，但这类方法往往产生"伪提示词"（pseudoprompts）——不可理解的、缺乏人类可解释性的token序列。作者认为这是因为提示词优化任务本身是病态的（ill-posed），需要新的框架来解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 将提示词优化任务重新定义为贝叶斯后验推断问题，即在给定目标答案/行为的条件下，对提示词的后验分布进行推断。在此基础上提出一种高效算法，从该后验分布中采样提示词，使采样结果在困惑度（效率）和人类可读性两方面同时取得良好表现。

Result: 在真实数据集上与当前最先进的方法进行比较，所提方法在多项评估指标上均表现出显著的改进，验证了该方法在生成既高效又可读的提示词方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯后验推断的视角重构提示词优化问题，可以避免传统困惑度最小化方法产生的伪提示词问题，在保持提示词效率的同时大幅提升其人类可解释性。

Abstract: Reconstructing prompts that can elicit a desired answer or behaviour in an LLM is an open and important research topic. Optimisation methods which aim at minimising the perplexity of a given answer, however, consistently yield so-called pseudoprompts, unintelligible strings of tokens which can lack human interpretability. We argue that this is a consequence of the ill-posedness of the prompt optimisation task. By reframing the task as a Bayesian posterior inference over prompts, we propose an efficient algorithm to sample prompts which are both efficient (in terms of perplexity) and human readable. We compare our approach with state of the art alternatives showing on a real data set a marked improvement over a range of metrics.

</details>


### [127] [BEAR-Bench: A Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17895)
*Liubov Chubarova,Alexandra Kuleshova,Daniil Volkov,Kirill Sultanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BEAR-Bench，一个基于英文和俄语文本密集型商业与科学文档的1000道人工标注题目的双语基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在复杂文档推理上的能力，并利用模型输出来比较现有的幻觉检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准要么侧重信息抽取，要么需要外部领域知识，要么将专业文档仅作为众多场景之一；且大多以英语或中文为主，俄语等语言严重缺乏代表性，导致MLLM在文本密集型专业文档上的推理能力未被充分评估。

Method: 构建一个包含1000道人工标注问题的BEAR-Bench基准，覆盖英文和俄语的商业与科学文本密集型文档；评估16个专有和开源MLLM（包括Gemini 3.1 Pro和Qwen3.5-397B）；并利用模型输出比较现有幻觉检测方法的可靠性。

Result: 在BEAR-Bench上评估16个MLLM后发现，即使最强系统也存在明显的性能提升空间；同时通过模型输出对现有幻觉检测方法进行了比较，评估了模型失败频率以及这些失败能否被可靠识别。

Conclusion: BEAR-Bench补充了现有基准在俄语和双语复杂文档推理评估上的空白，揭示了当前最强MLLM在专业文本密集型文档推理上的局限性，并为幻觉检测方法的可靠性提供了新见解。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual comprehension, their ability to reason about text-dense, professional documents remains incompletely evaluated. Existing benchmarks emphasize information extraction, require external domain knowledge, or cover professional documents only as one of many settings. They are also largely English- or Chinese-centric, leaving other languages and Russian, in particular, substantially underrepresented. To address these limitations, we introduce BEAR-Bench (Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning), a self-contained, complex English-and-Russian benchmark comprising 1000 human-annotated questions based on text-rich business and scientific documents. We evaluate 16 proprietary and open-weight MLLMs, including Gemini 3.1 Pro and Qwen3.5-397B, on BEAR-Bench and observe clear headroom even for the strongest systems. Finally, we use the resulting model outputs to compare existing hallucination detection methods, evaluating not only how often models fail on BEAR-Bench but also how reliably those failures can be identified.

</details>


### [128] [CABLE: Extending the Reach of Memory Retrieval via Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17911)
*Zheling Tan,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CABLE（基于前因的互补链接与扩展），一种即插即用的记忆增强方法。CABLE为每条新记忆生成前因导向的查询，检索历史记忆后减去语义近邻内容，再验证剩余结果并加入稀疏有向图，从而构建与宿主检索器互补的关联链接。检索时沿这些链接扩展检索种子以挖掘隐含支撑证据。在LoCoMo和MA-LongMemEval等基准上，CABLE在A-MEM、SimpleMem和Mem0g等多种记忆系统中均提升了LLM-judge得分，尤其在开放域、多会话和偏好类问题上收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长期间会话和结构化工作流中，即使保留完整历史，仅靠语义相似性检索往往无法在不同上下文中恢复早期经验、计划或动机等语义距离较远的证据。现有记忆图虽提供跨记忆结构，但其链接主要基于语义重叠驱动，容易与宿主检索器重复，未能真正扩展检索范围。

Method: CABLE采用两阶段设计：（1）在记忆写入时，为每条新记忆生成前因导向的查询，通过宿主检索器获取候选历史记忆，剔除语义直接近邻的候选，对剩余候选进行验证后将其作为互补关联加入稀疏有向图；（2）在检索时，对宿主系统检索到的种子节点沿该图进行扩展，揭示隐含的支撑证据。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MA-LongMemEval基准上，CABLE与A-MEM集成，并进一步集成到SimpleMem和Mem0g中，使用Qwen3.5-27B、DeepSeek-chat和GPT-4o-mini进行评测。CABLE在所有系统级设置中均获得更高的平均LLM-judge分数，最大增益出现在证据分散于多个记忆或会话的类别中，包括开放域、多会话和偏好导向问题。

Conclusion: 研究表明，记忆链接构建应优先考虑稀疏的、与推理相关的关联，而非简单地依赖语义重叠。这种检索器互补的链接策略能够有效提升长期间会话记忆的证据可达性，为LLM智能体的记忆系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate across structured workflows and sessions, preserving long-term history does not ensure that later contexts can recover relevant evidence through a bounded memory interface. We study this evidence-reachability problem in long-term conversational memory, where retrieval still relies heavily on semantic similarity. This works well for topical recall, but it often misses earlier experiences, plans, or motivations that are semantically distant from the later events they help explain. Existing memory graphs provide cross-memory structure, yet links driven mainly by semantic overlap can duplicate what the host retriever already recovers. We argue that link construction should instead prioritize a sparse set of retriever-complementary associations. We present CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion), a plug-in augmentation that constructs links designed to extend the host retriever's direct semantic reach. For each new memory, CABLE generates antecedent-oriented queries, retrieves prior memories, subtracts candidates in the direct semantic neighborhood, and verifies the remainder before adding the accepted complementary associations into a sparse directed graph. At retrieval time, CABLE expands the host system's retrieved seeds along these links to surface implicit supporting evidence. We evaluate CABLE with A-MEM on LoCoMo and MA-LongMemEval, and further integrate it into SimpleMem and Mem0g on LoCoMo, using Qwen3.5-27B, DeepSeek-chat, and GPT-4o-mini. CABLE yields higher mean LLM-judge scores in every evaluated system-level setting, with the largest gains in categories where useful evidence is distributed across memories or sessions, including open-domain, multi-session, and preference-oriented questions. These results support prioritizing sparse, reasoning-relevant associations that complement rather than duplicate the host retriever.

</details>


### [129] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechSense，一个用于细粒度语音情感分析的新型数据集，专门定义8类人际立场（如自信、不耐烦）用于捕捉超越词汇内容的韵律线索，并通过实验证明具有声学信息访问的模型优于纯文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析研究存在两个主要局限：一是主流方法依赖文本为中心的pipeline（ASR+文本分析），丢失韵律和语调等关键声学特征；二是现有基准在标签粒度上存在不匹配，优先考虑基本情感（如快乐、悲伤）而非社交敏感性所必需的细腻人际立场（如自信、不耐烦）。

Method: 提出SpeechSense数据集：定义一个基于韵律线索（超越词汇内容）可检测的8类人际立场专门分类体系，基于该分类法构建数据集，采用高保真语音合成和严格的人工验证。

Result: 在多种多模态LLM、纯文本LLM和语音编码器上的综合实验表明，具有声学访问能力的模型始终优于纯文本基线，实证验证了声学线索在检测微妙说话者态度中的首要地位。

Conclusion: 实验结果强调了声学线索在细粒度语音情感分析中的关键作用，验证了SpeechSense数据集的必要性，并为该领域提供了新的基准资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [130] [Grading Needs a Rubric, Not Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17938)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"any-to-bench"框架：由前沿模型在文档摄取阶段一次性提取试题与评分量规，随后由低成本模型基于显式量规重复执行所有评分任务。实验表明，低成本模型在基于量规评分时与昂贵模型可靠性相当，答案身份解释了95.6%的分数方差，而评分者身份仅占0.2%。消融实验揭示，量规中"参考答案"部分承担了绝大部分评分决策作用，移除它会导致可靠性崩溃（ICC从0.888降至0.628）并使评分者推理能力重新变得重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）自动评分开放式考试答案已被广泛研究，但现有方法通常依赖昂贵的前沿模型执行重复评分任务，成本高昂。作者希望验证一个核心假设：当评分依据明确的量规（rubric）而非模型内在智能时，小型廉价模型可以媲美甚至超越昂贵模型的评分可靠性。这一假设若成立，可大幅降低大规模教育评估的成本。

Method: 提出any-to-bench架构：（1）摄取阶段，前沿模型通读源文档一次，提取每道试题及其评分量规；（2）评分阶段，低成本模型依据提取的量规重复评分所有答卷。实验设计包括：两个模型家族（六种成本高效配置）×三个推理努力水平，每个配置回答24道开放式考试题，并对每份答卷评分三次，共产生3,456个评分。采用方差分解分析分数变异的来源（答案身份vs评分者身份），并纳入六个前沿模型作为对照。此外进行两组消融实验：移除量规的标准与等级（保留参考答案）、同时移除参考答案，以分解量规各组分的贡献。

Result: （1）答案身份解释95.6%的分数方差，评分者身份仅解释0.2%；（2）提高答题者的推理努力最高使得分变动0.143满分比，而提高评分者的推理努力最多仅影响0.006；（3）六个前沿模型作为评分小组并未展现出更高可靠性；（4）消融一：移除量规标准与等级但保留参考答案，评分结果几乎无变化；（5）消融二：进一步移除参考答案后，ICC从0.888骤降至0.628，分数膨胀，且评分者推理努力重新变得重要；（6）未发现长度偏好或同模型家族偏好。

Conclusion: 量规是将评分行为与评分者智能解耦的关键机制；而在量规内部，"参考答案"承担了绝大部分决策工作。实际含义是：构建高质量评分系统时，应把主要精力投入到参考答案的编写与维护上，而非采购更强、更昂贵的模型。前沿模型只需在摄取阶段使用一次，后续所有重复性评分工作均可由低成本模型完成，且可靠性不打折扣。

Abstract: Small language models can grade open-ended examination answers as reliably as substantially more expensive models when they grade against an explicit rubric. We test this claim as the design principle behind any-to-bench: a frontier model reads source documents once, at ingestion, to extract each question and its rubric; lower-cost models then perform all repeated grading work. We evaluate six cost-efficient model configurations from two model families at three reasoning-effort levels. Each configuration answers 24 open-ended examination questions, and each also grades every answer sheet three times, yielding 3,456 per-question grades. Scores depend overwhelmingly on the answer being graded: answer identity explains 95.6% of score variance, whereas judge identity explains only 0.2%. Raising a writer's reasoning effort moves earned scores by as much as 0.143 of full marks, while raising a judge's reasoning effort moves assigned scores by at most 0.006. Six frontier-tier judges, added as a check, reproduce these scores and are no more reliable as a panel. Two ablations then decompose the rubric on the same questions and answers. Removing its criteria and levels while keeping the official answer changes nothing measurable. Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [131] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文绕过注意力权重，直接从隐藏状态流形的动态几何出发，证明深层LLM潜在空间天然组织为小世界网络，并发现深层推理层存在尖锐的拓扑相变，将巨大概念距离压缩为不超过6跳的可导航路径。该框架还被用于RAG中的零样本幻觉检测，事实性生成与其源上下文保持约3跳的结构完整性，而幻觉则导致严重的拓扑坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于注意力的可解释性方法因注意力汇聚（attention sinks）等路由伪影，无法捕捉真正的语义邻近性，难以理解LLM在长上下文中执行多跳推理的内部机制。作者希望绕过注意力权重，从隐藏状态流形的几何结构出发，直接揭示深层推理的底层组织原理。

Method: 将长上下文表示形成的连续相似性矩阵稀疏化为无权重图，跨两种不同架构追踪高度不相关语义锚点之间的连通性，从而分析隐藏状态流形的小世界网络特性与拓扑相变。并将该几何框架应用于RAG场景下的零样本幻觉检测。

Result: 发现早期句法层完全断裂，而深层推理层发生尖锐的拓扑相变，将大规模概念距离压缩为严格受"六度分隔"（<=6语义跳）限制的可导航路径。幻觉检测方面，事实性生成与源上下文约3跳，而幻觉导致严重的拓扑坍塌。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上形式化了Transformer执行抽象推理的机制，提供了一种严格的几何特征来评估事实可靠性，为LLM内部机制的可解释性打开了新的视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [132] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AVShift是首个德语作者身份验证基准，包含超过15万文本对，覆盖三种体裁和21年时间跨度，在统一框架下评估跨体裁、时间及AI时代的数据分布偏移。实验表明微调后的LLM跨体裁泛化最佳，时间漂移是最强的影响因素，而AI时代未检测到可测量的分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AV基准通常孤立地研究体裁、时间和AI辅助写作等分布偏移因素，且主要集中在英语上，导致对模型在现实条件下鲁棒性的理解有限。作者身份验证在实践中面临结合多个分布偏移的复杂场景，需要一个统一的德语基准来系统性评估。

Method: 构建AVShift基准，包含超过15万文本对、三种体裁、21年时间跨度，支持在统一框架内进行跨体裁、时间及AI时代的受控评估；测评基于特征、基于嵌入和基于LLM的代表性方法。

Result: 微调LLM在跨体裁上泛化最佳，且从风格多样的训练数据中获益显著；时间漂移是影响AV最强的因素之一，文档时间间隔越大性能下降越明显；未发现AI时代分布偏移的可测量证据；特征分析揭示了跨体裁稳定的风格特征，但其相对重要性随体裁转换而变化。

Conclusion: AVShift为德语环境下多分布偏移的AV评估提供了统一基准，揭示了时间偏移的重要性、LLM方法的优势及稳定的风格特征，并公开了基准和代码以促进未来研究。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个风险受控的框架，用于在无参考答案的客观任务中让LLM作为评判者。该框架通过在留出集上校准不确定性阈值，利用Clopper-Pearson区间确保被接受判决的假发现率（FDR）低于用户指定水平α。当参数化模式置信度不足时，实例被路由到检索增强模式，通过网页证据重新评估。该框架在开放域问答基准上保持目标错误率，同时覆盖率显著高于单模式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者已成为标准实践，但客观任务中存在独特的可靠性挑战：无参考答案时，评判者依赖参数化知识（可能产生幻觉或缺乏证据）或工具增强（增加计算成本且需要可靠的使用机制）。更重要的是，两种方法单独使用都无法对接受判决的风险提供正式控制。

Method: 提出风险受控框架，在留出集上校准不确定性阈值，使用有限样本Clopper-Pearson区间保证被接受判决的假发现率低于用户指定水平α（概率较高）。采用双阈值路由机制：参数化模式置信度不足时，路由到检索增强模式，通过网页证据收集并重新评估，第二个校准阈值下进行判断，且有限样本保证无需额外假设即可迁移。

Result: 在开放域QA基准和不同规模评判模型上，该框架维持目标错误率的同时，覆盖率显著高于单模式基线（即纯参数化或纯检索增强模式）。

Conclusion: 通过双模式路由和有限样本统计校准，可以在无参考答案的客观任务中实现对LLM评判者接受判决的正式风险控制，兼顾可靠性、覆盖率和计算效率。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IOL-AI挑战赛，一个在2026年国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛个人赛未公开题目上进行的开源竞赛，首次由官方IOL评审团按照人类参赛者标准对AI系统进行评分。比赛有731份提交、46支队伍参与，计算资源严格受限（单块T4、30分钟）。结果发现Claude Opus 4.8获得金牌等效分数，而资源受限系统仅处于人类参赛者后5%水平；能力不受规模决定（14B参数模型可超越两倍规模模型），增益来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量；自动指标与评审团排名一致但压缩了分数差距；前沿模型对问题语言的前瞻知识并不能显著帮助解决语言推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理研究集中于数学和代码等提供明确规则的领域，而语言谜题要求解题者先发现语言系统再进行推理，是一种更全面的推理能力测试。此前AI在IOL题目上的评估缺乏官方评审依据，且尚未在严格计算约束条件下系统研究语言推理能力，因此需要建立一个基于官方IOL标准、能反映真实推理能力的基准。

Method: 在IOL 2026个人赛未公开题目上举办开源竞赛，采用双重评估：自动指标评分与官方IOL评审团按人类参赛者标准评分。设置严格计算预算（单块T4 GPU、30分钟），同时额外评测15个不受资源约束的前沿模型和开源模型，并进行对比分析。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8获得评审团评分相当于金牌水平；资源受限系统仅处于人类参赛者底部5%范围。14B参数模型表现优于两倍规模模型，性能提升主要来自解码策略和输出处理而非模型容量。自动指标与评审团排名一致但压缩分数尺度，弱系统被高估约13分。前沿模型对问题语言的前瞻知识对解题能力无显著帮助。

Conclusion: 语言推理可作为通用推理能力的强基准代理。规模并非决定因素，解码与输出处理策略更为关键。自动评估可替代人工评审用于排名，但需校正分数压缩偏差。当前资源受限的AI系统在语言学推理上仍远落后于顶尖人类表现。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出链式经验（Chain-of-Experience, CoE）概念，研究大语言模型在测试阶段通过多轮迭代交互积累经验以持续改进的能力，实验表明该方法在多个领域和多种反馈机制下均优于无反馈基线，同时降低API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估忽视了模型通过推理时交互迭代改进的能力，而人类从经验中持续学习。论文旨在研究LLM在测试时通过迭代经验积累实现持续改进的机制。

Method: 提出CoE框架，通过多种反馈机制（模型自我反馈、正确性信号、公开编码测试通过率等环境信号）在测试阶段进行迭代交互，形成持续改进循环。实验涵盖数学、编码、知识三个领域，使用8个LLM（包括GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet）进行评估。

Result: 迭代经验利用一致优于无反馈基线；仅自我反馈即可获得显著提升，总体改进5.6%，API成本降低19%；结合互补反馈通道可获得额外收益；CoE在每token准确率上优于现有测试时策略；LLM基础能力与改进能力正相关；模型在弱或虚假反馈下保持鲁棒性；不同反馈贡献不同改进方面；大部分收益在迭代早期涌现。

Conclusion: CoE展示了LLM通过测试时迭代交互持续改进的可行性，为测试时计算和LLM评估范式提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言中存在第二个参数"相位"(phase)，它不同于词嵌入和注意力权重所捕获的"振幅"(关联强度)。相位仅存在于意义之间，决定共激活意义如何组合，甚至可以在意义完全在场的情况下反转其贡献。作者主张弱版本：解释需要一个有符号的、持久的、索引到个体和二元组的关系参数；标准Transformer在冻结推理中没有相位的显式表示，可解释性追求单义性的方向与此相悖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型基于词共现统计和注意力权重来估计关联强度（振幅），但这些方法将所有作者混在一起进行聚合，删除了历史索引的相位信息。作者认为这种群体平均化只能识别总体边际状态，无法确定任何个体或二元状态，因此需要第二个参数来捕捉意义组合的方式，特别是反讽、暗示、引用等依赖意义共存条件的现象。

Method: 论文从理论上论证相位参数的必要性：通过语言形式可以在信号中设置相位；遭遇经历会安装相位关系；历史会分布这些关系。作者提出了六个可检验的预测，涵盖被抑制意义是否仍保持活跃、遭遇顺序是否改变短语的功能、标记信号是否改变共享短语的理解方式、以及模型面对历史时是被改变还是仅被告知。理论要求的架构是带有主体索引、携带相位的语义状态的语言模型。

Result: 作者明确辩护的是弱版本论断：解释需要第二个关系参数（有符号的、持久的、以个体和二元组为索引）。量子概率只是该参数的一种记法，并非主张大脑中存在量子过程。强版本（量子演算比有符号经典模型更能约束这些现象）尚未推导出遭遇顺序约束，因此仍悬而未决。

Conclusion: 标准Transformer缺乏用于冻结推理的相位显式表示，而可解释性研究以单义性为进展指标实际上违背了相位逻辑——它视为缺陷的共存恰恰是暗示、反讽和引用得以成立的条件。未来方向是构建具有主体索引和相位承载语义状态的语言模型。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokEval框架，一个超越传统指标（如fertility和压缩率）的tokenizer评估指标集，包含UTF-8字符边界完整性和数字位值边界对齐等结构与语言相关属性。通过控制变量预训练实验，发现信息论指标可预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho最高0.80），而结构敏感指标（如数字和换行处理）与任务准确率相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型tokenizer通常只经过最简评估就被选定，但其设计选择直接影响模型能力。目前对tokenizer属性与下游性能之间关系的理解有限，缺乏系统性的评估框架，导致tokenizer选择往往依赖经验或昂贵的预训练扫描。

Method: 提出TokEval指标体系，捕捉超越标准指标的语义和结构属性（UTF-8字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐等）。随后进行受控预训练实验，仅变动tokenizer的训练数据混合、预分词策略和训练算法，在bits-per-byte和多个基准（语言理解、数学推理、代码生成）上评估模型，并通过Spearman相关性分析内部属性指标与下游表现的关系。

Result: 实验表明不同内在属性对不同模型能力有不同影响：信息论指标能预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho高达0.80），而结构敏感指标（如数字和换行符处理）与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval有望实现更原理化的tokenizer评估，在内在测量与下游性能一致的场景下，用内在测量替代昂贵的预训练扫描，从而简化tokenizer的选择流程。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射学报告结构化和质量控制（QA）。该系统对638份CT报告（22,270个句子）进行句子级结构化标注，并检测发现节与印象节不一致、性别-解剖冲突及关键发现未记录等问题。放射科医生评估显示69%的报告被正确重构，无临床重要信息遗漏或虚构内容，84%的QA性能被评为"优秀"或"良好"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告在临床实践中存在格式不统一、内容不一致等问题，可能影响临床决策和患者安全。现有的报告结构化方法多依赖人工或单一模型，缺乏自动化质量控制手段，因此需要开发一个能够同时完成报告结构化和质量保证的集成系统。

Method: 采用本地部署的多智能体AI流水线，包含两个核心功能：(1) 报告结构化——通过正则规则和本地大语言模型（LLM）在句子层级将报告划分为标准解剖学章节；(2) 质量保证——自动检测Findings与Impression章节之间的不匹配、章节内不一致、性别-解剖冲突，以及关键发现的未记录沟通。由两名经委员会认证的放射科医生对45份报告子集进行独立评估。

Result: 系统成功将所有22,270个句子的Findings章节结构化到预设解剖格式。标记了90份（14.1%）报告存在问题，最常见的是章节不匹配（80份，12.5%）。放射科医生评估中，两名评审一致认为69%的报告被正确重构、4%不正确、27%存在分歧；一致同意无临床重要信息遗漏或虚构内容；84%的QA性能被评为"优秀"或"良好"，其余为"一般"。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统能够在一个统一工作流程中同时实现放射学报告结构化和质量保证，在放射科医生评估中表现良好。该类系统可能有助于推动放射实践中的报告标准化和质量保证。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [RADmesh: Remesh-Aware Mesh Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17182)
*Nam Anh Dinh,Itai Lang,Oded Stein,Rana Hanocka*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合重网格化的生成式网格变形方法，通过周期性的各向同性重网格化来保持元素质量，从而支持大尺度、鲁棒于视觉损失噪声的变形，并实现从粗到细的自适应分辨率增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本、视觉监督的生成式网格变形方法在保持三角剖分不变的情况下进行大幅形变会严重损害元素质量；重网格化虽是离散操作，但与视觉损失的噪声监督信号难以结合，因此需要一种能耦合两者的方法。

Method: 提出顶点级别的变形优化量，能够支持大变形并鲁棒于视觉损失噪声；周期性调用各向同性重网格化器，该重网格化器插值并延续变形优化状态，从而在从粗到细的网格分辨率增长过程中保持几何信息驱动的连续优化，并具有局部性（可只生长局部新特征而不影响其余区域）。

Result: 在多种形状和提示（局部及全局变形）上验证了方法的有效性，展示了更优的视觉质量和三角形效率，生成的三角剖分贴合优化几何并具有规整的各向同性元素。

Conclusion: 重网格化能显著提升生成式形状变形的质量与鲁棒性，所提方法在视觉质量和三角形效率上均优于现有方法，是一种可局部化、分辨率自适应的变形方案。

Abstract: We propose a remeshing-enhanced method for generatively deforming shapes with visual losses. It is intuitive that sufficiently drastic deformations of a mesh without changing its triangulation can easily compromise element quality, even if such large geometry changes may be semantically desired. Shape deformation methods could thus benefit from changing the triangulation; however, this is not done by most generative, text-based, visually-supervised mesh deformation methods. Remeshing is a discrete operation, proven to be especially challenging to couple with the notoriously noisy supervision signal provided by visual losses. We propose a vertex-based deformation optimization quantity capable of large deformations and robustness to such noise; we periodically remesh using an isotropic remesher that interpolates and carries forward the deformation optimization state. This enables continuous, geometry-informed progress in coarse-to-fine addition of resolution. The resulting shapes' triangulations fit their optimized geometry and have neat isotropic elements. Further, our method is localizable, able to grow new features on a base shape with expressive detail, leaving the rest unchanged. We showcase the effectiveness of our method on a variety of shapes and prompts, both local and global deformations, and demonstrate its superior visual quality and triangle efficiency. Our project page is at https://threedle.github.io/radmesh.

</details>


### [140] [3D Gaussian Accelerated Ray Tracing: Fast training through particle-based backward propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17298)
*Laurent Vit,Oliver Batchelor,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出3DGART，一个面向光线追踪高斯渲染的实用训练框架。其核心创新在于将反向传播从像素中心重组为图元中心，通过保守的透视校正屏幕空间边界构建紧凑中间缓冲区和瓦片-图元映射，将梯度计算从高竞争的散射操作转化为结构化的收集过程。在Mip-NeRF 360上相较逐像素基线和3DGRT分别获得约3-3.5倍和约4倍的训练加速，同时提升渲染质量，使完全光线追踪的高斯训练达到与光栅化管线相当的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射（3DGS）因其光栅化公式依赖屏幕空间近似，限制了视点相关排序的准确性以及反射、折射、阴影等次级光线效果的集成。高斯光线追踪（GRT）虽能解决这些问题，但训练成本高昂——主要瓶颈并非光线遍历本身，而是像素中心的反向传播中多个线程并发向同一图元参数累积梯度，导致严重的原子竞争和线程串行化。

Method: 提出3DGART框架，核心是将反向传播围绕图元而非像素进行重组。具体而言：利用保守的透视校正屏幕空间边界构建紧凑的中间缓冲区，并建立瓦片-图元（tile-primitive）映射，使每个线程在一个瓦片内累积单个图元在其覆盖像素上的贡献，从而将梯度计算从高竞争的散射（scatter）操作转化为结构化的收集（gather）式过程。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360数据集上，3DGART相比逐像素基线获得约3-3.5倍原始训练加速，相比3DGRT获得约4倍加速，同时提升了渲染质量。更重要的是，它使完全光线追踪的高斯训练变得实用，运行时间可与光栅化管线的训练相竞争，并保留了光线追踪的优势。

Conclusion: 3DGART通过重新设计反向传播的计算组织方式，成功解决了光线追踪高斯渲染训练中的原子竞争瓶颈，使高质量的光线追踪效果不再以训练效率为代价，为高斯渲染在光线追踪方向上的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has made Gaussian primitives a highly efficient representation for real-time novel view synthesis, but its rasterisation-based formulation relies on screen-space approximations that limit accurate view-dependent ordering and the integration of secondary ray effects such as reflections, refractions, and shadows. Gaussian ray tracing addresses these limitations by evaluating explicit ray-primitive intersections, yet it remains costly to train. We observe that the main bottleneck is not ray traversal alone, but the pixel-centric backward propagation, where many threads concurrently accumulate gradients into the same primitive parameters, causing severe atomic contention and thread serialisation.
  We present 3DGART, a practical training framework for ray-traced Gaussian rendering. Our key idea is to reorganise backward propagation around primitives rather than pixels. Using conservative perspective-correct screen-space bounds, we build a compact intermediate buffer and a tile-primitive mapping that allows each thread to accumulate the contribution of one primitive over its covered pixels within a tile. This transforms gradient computation from a contention-heavy scatter operation into a structured gather-like process. On Mip-NeRF 360, 3DGART achieves an $\approx 3-3.5\times$ raw training speedup over per-pixel baseline and $\approx4 \times$ over 3DGRT on Mip-NeRF 360 while improving quality. More importantly, 3DGART makes fully ray-traced Gaussian training practical, reaching runtimes competitive with rasterisation-based pipelines while preserving benefits of ray tracing.

</details>


### [141] [Six Ways to Draw Vangers with WebGPU: Real-Time Rendering of Editable Multi-Layer Height Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17390)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文在单引擎、单数据路径下对六种地形渲染方法（高度场射线步进、体素加速射线步进、切片代理几何、逐样本条形光栅化、计算散射、拟合三角网格）进行了公平对比，测试对象是Vangers (1998)手工创作的多层地形。所有方法都须同时支持两个实体间隔、交互帧率渲染以及局部地形破坏编辑。结果表明，在特定质量设置下，贪婪TIN平均帧时最低，但其拟合成本由第二层而非地表起伏决定，且可编辑网格需占用319 MiB GPU与535 MiB CPU资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地形LOD研究几乎都基于DEM（数字高程模型）展开，而DEM是单值、采样尺度平滑、真实地形采样得到的。游戏地形往往不具备这些特性——它可能是多层、不连续、手工创作的。因此需要专门研究适合游戏多层地形的渲染方法，并做系统性的公平对比。

Method: 在单一引擎中用统一WGSL/WebGPU API实现六种渲染方法：高度场射线步进、体素加速射线步进、切片代理几何、逐样本条形光栅化、计算散射、拟合三角网格（贪心TIN）。所有方法共享单一数据路径，以CPU光线投射同一源数据作为评分基准。测试三种工况：保留地面样本处两个实体间隔、交互帧率、支持无重载的局部地形破坏。对比原游戏俯视角与眼平视角下的效果差异，并在多设备上测量帧时。

Result: 六种方法在俯视角下表现几乎无差异，但在眼平地平线视角下差异显著：点散射丢失覆盖、切片出现条带、过度简化网格可能漏墙。在所选质量设置下，贪心TIN平均帧时在所有测试设备上最低；但TIN拟合成本由第二层而非地表起伏决定，且可编辑网格需319 MiB GPU三角化缓存和535 MiB CPU三角化数据。同时发布了引擎、测试平台和一命令测量协议。

Conclusion: 基于DEM的传统LOD方法不能直接套用于游戏多层地形场景；多层实体间隔与可编辑性对视锥裁剪、缓存策略和网格预计算提出了额外约束。贪心TIN在当前设置下综合最优，但内存/拟合开销需权衡。研究为游戏地形渲染方法选择提供了可复现的评测框架与基准。

Abstract: Terrain level-of-detail is measured almost exclusively on digital elevation models: single-valued, smooth at the sampling scale, sampled from real topography. Game terrain is often none of these. We compare six rendering methods - height-field ray marching, voxel-accelerated ray marching, sliced proxy geometry, per-sample bar rasterization, compute scattering, and a fitted triangle mesh - implemented in a single engine over a single data path, on the hand-authored multi-layer terrain of Vangers (1998), scored against a CPU ray cast of the same source data. Every method must preserve the two solid intervals available at a ground sample, render at interactive rates, and reflect local terrain destruction without reloading the level. These constraints rule out treating caves as decoration or amortizing a static preprocessing step over an immutable map.
  From the original game's top-down camera the six methods look interchangeable. At eye-level horizons they do not: point scattering loses coverage, slicing bands, and an over-simplified mesh can miss a wall. At the selected quality settings a greedy triangulated irregular network (TIN) has the lowest mean frame time on every device we measured, but the fit cost is set by the second layer rather than by floor relief, and making that mesh editable retains 319 MiB of GPU geometry and 535 MiB of CPU triangulation. All six implementations use the same native wgpu / WebGPU API and canonical WGSL. We release the engine, the harness, and a one-command measurement protocol.

</details>


### [142] [Variational r-Adaptive Cloth Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17833)
*Jiahao Wen,Zhen Chen,Jernej Barbič,Danny M. Kaufman*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出首个用于布料动力学与静力学摩擦接触仿真的r-自适应方法，通过退化激活的质量正则化解决IP优化的局部极小和单元塌缩问题，并引入动态非线性求解器实现3-6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄布料需要高空间分辨率以复现褶皱、折叠、屈曲和尖锐接触特征，但现有变分r-自适应方法应用于分片线性壳时面临两个耦合的失效模式：IP优化可能陷入不良局部极小值导致物理配置次优；单元塌缩会人工降低IP，使目标函数依赖的有限元近似失效。

Method: 提出退化激活的质量正则化（degeneracy-activated quality regularization），对形状良好的单元保持非激活以保留各向异性自适应和局部加密，但在接近退化时增强作用以抑制虚假低能量盆地、改善从次优物理极小值的逃逸，并防止单元聚集（布料在尖锐接触特征上滑动时单元逐步塌缩的特殊失效模式）。同时引入动态非线性求解器，利用步内相干性加速导数评估和动态IPC容差更新，用于r-自适应迭代信任域（ITR）求解。

Result: 在挑战性的摩擦接触场景实验中，动态求解器相对先前最优ITR实现3-6倍加速；在相等顶点数和时间预算约束下，该方法比固定网格获得更高的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了r-自适应布料仿真中的局部极小和单元退化问题，显著提升求解效率，并在相同资源约束下优于固定网格方法的视觉质量。

Abstract: We present the first r-adaptive method for simulating cloth dynamics and statics with frictional contact in modern cloth pipelines. Thin cloth requires high effective spatial resolution to reproduce wrinkles, folds, buckling, and sharp contact features. However, applying existing variational r-adaptivity to piecewise-linear shells reveals two coupled failure modes. Discretized incremental-potential (IP) optimization can become trapped in poor local minima, yielding suboptimal physical configurations. It can also lower IP artificially by collapsing elements, invalidating the finite-element approximation on which the objective relies. We address both problems with degeneracy-activated quality regularization. The regularizer remains inactive for well-shaped elements, preserving anisotropic adaptation and local densification, but becomes strong near degeneracy. It suppresses spurious low-energy basins, improves escape from suboptimal physical minima, and prevents element bunching, a cloth-specific failure in which elements progressively collapse as cloth slides across sharp contact features. For practical performance, we introduce a dynamic nonlinear solver that exploits within-timestep coherence through accelerated derivative evaluation and dynamic IPC tolerance updates for r-adaptive iterative trust-region (ITR) solves. This yields a 3-6x speedup over prior optimal ITR. Experiments on challenging frictional-contact scenarios show that, under equal vertex-count and time-budget constraints, our method achieves higher visual fidelity than fixed meshes.

</details>


### [143] [Love Handles: Decimation for Deformation Handles with Compact Support and Low Memory Footprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17930)
*David IW Levin,Paul Kry,Kartic Subr,Ryan Schmidt,Etienne Vouga,Teseo Schneider*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于抽取（decimation）的算法，用于计算稀疏、紧致支撑的形变手柄（deformation handles），通过迭代代数简化优化手柄形变以匹配任意输入形变（如线性振动模态），并利用紧致支撑实现高效的缩减立方体（reduced-cubature）计算方案，从而在复杂几何上实现实时弹性动力学仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体形变的物理仿真计算开销大，当细化物体表示（增加离散化程度）时扩展性差。尽管简化阶数方法（ROM）通过减少自由度可以节省计算量，但如何高效地计算稀疏且紧致支撑的形变手柄仍是一个挑战。

Method: 1. 提出首个基于抽取（decimation）的算法来计算稀疏、紧致支撑的形变手柄集合；2. 核心是迭代代数简化，优化手柄形变以匹配任意输入形变（如线性振动模态）；3. 适用于任何体积输入网格（包括高亏格或多孔特征），不改动几何；4. 设计高效算法计算和更新紧致支撑及其权重；5. 利用紧致支撑开发高效的缩减立方体（reduced-cubature）计算方案。

Result: 优化后的手柄提供内存高效的解决方案，实现对复杂几何的实时弹性动力学仿真，在多达796,623个四面体的多种四面体网格上展示了实时性能。

Conclusion: 该算法能够在保持几何不变的前提下，为任意体积网格生成稀疏且紧致支撑的形变手柄，并通过缩减立方体计算实现复杂几何的实时弹性动力学仿真，为计算机动画、工程和机器人领域的形变仿真提供了高效的工具。

Abstract: Estimating the deformation of solids via physical simulation is an important problem spanning fields such as computer animation, engineering and robotics. Such simulations are computationally expensive and scale poorly when the representation of an object is refined by increasing the level of discretization. Reduced Order Methods (ROM) offer computational savings by decreasing the number of degrees of freedom, for example by using \emph{handles} that control groups of vertices. We present the first decimation-based algorithm for computing a sparse, compactly supported set of deformation handles. The crux of our method utilizes iterative algebraic simplification to optimize handle deformation to match any input deformation, such as linear vibration modes. This applies to any volumetric input mesh, including those with high genus or porous features, since we do not alter the geometry. We also devise an efficient algorithm to compute and update compact supports and their associated weights. We leverage compact support to develop an efficient, reduced-cubature computation scheme. Once optimized, our handles offer a memory-efficient solution while enabling real-time elastodynamics simulation of complex geometry. We show real-time performance on a variety of tetrahedral meshes with up to 796,623 tetrahedra.

</details>


### [144] [MetaSapiens v2: Advancing Real-Time Foveated Neural Rendering via Foveation-Aware Pruning and Stereo Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17969)
*Weikai Lin,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MetaSapiens v2是一个面向VR/AR设备的实时点基神经渲染系统，通过效率感知剪枝、注视点渲染、选择性双目扭曲和专用加速器四种技术组合，实现比现有PBNR模型高一个数量级的加速，同时保持人类视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点基神经渲染（PBNR）因其实时、逼真渲染能力在AR/VR和数字孪生领域需求旺盛，但在VR/AR设备上实现实时PBNR面临巨大挑战，现有方法难以同时满足实时性和视觉质量要求。

Method: MetaSapiens v2结合四种技术：(1) 效率感知剪枝技术优化渲染速度；(2) 针对PBNR的高效图元注视点渲染（FR）方法，利用人眼周边视觉灵敏度低的特性放宽周边渲染质量；(3) 利用双目冗余的选择性扭曲方法减少双目渲染计算开销；(4) 面向双目FR的加速器设计，解决FR-based PBNR中的负载不均衡问题并支持扭曲以支持高效双目渲染。

Result: 评估表明MetaSapiens v2相比现有PBNR模型实现了数量级（一个数量级）的加速，同时保持人类视觉质量。

Conclusion: MetaSapiens v2通过系统级协同优化（算法与硬件协同设计），成功将PBNR实时渲染能力落地到VR/AR设备上，在显著提升渲染速度的同时维持了人眼感知质量。

Abstract: Point-Based Neural Rendering (PBNR) is emerging as a promising class of rendering techniques, which are permeating all aspects of society, driven by a growing demand for real-time, photorealistic rendering in AR/VR and digital twins. However, achieving real-time PBNR on VR/AR devices is challenging. This paper proposes MetaSapiens v2, a PBNR system that delivers real-time neural rendering on VR/AR devices while maintaining human visual quality. MetaSapiens v2 combines four techniques. First, we present an efficiency-aware pruning technique to optimize rendering speed. Second, we introduce a Foveated Rendering (FR) method with an efficient primitive for PBNR, leveraging humans' low visual acuity in peripheral regions to relax rendering quality and improve rendering speed. Third, we leverage the redundancy between the two eyes and propose a selective warping method to further reduce the computation overhead in AR/VR binocular rendering. Finally, we propose an accelerator design for binocular FR, addressing the load imbalance issue in (FR-based) PBNR and supporting warping for efficient binocular rendering. Our evaluation shows that MetaSapiens v2 achieves an order of magnitude speedup over existing PBNR models while maintaining the visual quality.

</details>


### [145] [aDSL: Agentic 3D Creation via Joint Agent-Program Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17975)
*Rui-Huan Wang,Si-Tong Wei,Jia-Qi He,Heng-Yi Wei,Baoquan Chen,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种智能体为中心的领域特定语言（aDSL）与角色专业化多智能体系统协同设计的方法，通过将几何操作从脆弱的绝对坐标转向关系运算符，并采用Plan-Execute-Critic循环，显著提升了LLM在3D程序化内容生成中的鲁棒性和对用户意图的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖LLM编写3D程序的智能体工作流十分脆弱，难以将高层意图转化为一致的底层几何。作者认为根本原因在于现有程序化接口与LLM推理优势（善于语义结构和空间关系，不擅长脆弱的数值选择）之间存在不匹配。

Method: 作者提出了协同设计的两个核心组件：(1) aDSL——一种面向智能体的领域特定语言，强调可组合性和空间推理，通过关系运算符而非绝对坐标来操作几何；(2) 角色专业化的多智能体系统，遵循Plan-Execute-Critic循环，将请求分解为子任务、合成代码，并利用执行反馈迭代修复错误和约束违反。该方法无需训练。

Result: 实验表明，该协同设计方法在文本到形状和图像到形状任务上优于先前的LLM基线方法，同时保持了显式结构、可编辑性和可解释性。此外还支持铰接物体创建和结构化场景组合等下游应用。

Conclusion: 通过将DSL设计与多智能体推理流程进行协同设计，可以有效弥合高层语义意图与底层几何生成之间的鸿沟，为3D程序化内容创建提供更稳健、可控且忠实于用户意图的解决方案。

Abstract: Programmatic representations provide a compelling paradigm for 3D content creation, enabling fine-grained edits, interpretability, and explicit structural control. Yet, agentic workflows that rely on large language models (LLMs) to author 3D programs remain brittle, often failing to translate high-level intent into consistent low-level geometry. We attribute this fragility to a mismatch between existing programmatic interfaces and the reasoning strengths of LLMs, which favor semantic structure and spatial relations over fragile numeric choices. In this paper, we jointly design an Agent-centric Domain-Specific Language (aDSL) and a role-specialized multi-agent system to close this gap. aDSL bridges semantic logic and geometric constraints by emphasizing composability and spatial reasoning; it enables agents to manipulate geometry through relational operators instead of brittle absolute coordinates. Building on aDSL, our training-free multi-agent system follows a Plan-Execute-Critic loop to decompose requests, synthesize code, and iteratively repair errors and constraint violations using execution feedback. Experiments show that this co-design improves robustness, controllability, and faithfulness to user intent. Our method outperforms prior LLM-based baselines on text-to-shape and image-to-shape tasks while preserving explicit structure, editability, and interpretability. It also enables downstream applications such as articulated object creation and structured scene composition. Our code is available at https://github.com/sig-pku/aDSL.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent，一个基于DAG拓扑的多智能体系统，用于CDISC标准下的临床试验编程。在CDISC-Bench基准上，GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6在三次独立运行中实现100%结构化匹配（49/49变量，254条记录），显著优于最佳RAG基线（59.2%）和所有单智能体/扁平多智能体方法（0%）。DAG拓扑还能赋能弱模型，GPT-4.1在同样DAG下从0%提升到59.2%。该方法可推广到ADAE数据集，首次尝试即达100%匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床编程（将研究方案转化为符合CDISC标准的分析就绪数据集）是监管提交中的关键瓶颈。尽管LLM代码生成在许多领域表现出色，但在这一任务上所有前沿模型均彻底失败（11次单次尝试全部无法生成有效的受试者级分析数据集）。作者旨在解决LLM在此特定领域推理能力不足的问题，探索是否可通过编码领域流程知识而非依赖LLM自身推理来实现可靠输出。

Method: GxP-Agent将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），将整体数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点，由具有pharmaverse技能上下文的工作代理（worker agents）执行，配备验证门控（validation gates）和条件重试（conditional retry）机制。作者还构建了CDISC-Bench，一个基于FDA试点提交CDISCPilot01（254名受试者，49个真实ADSL变量）的执行型基准。实验对比了单智能体、扁平多智能体、RAG增强基线和GxP-Agent在多种模型上的性能，并验证了DAG拓扑对弱模型（如GPT-4.1）的赋能效果，同时将该方法推广到更复杂的ADAE（不良事件，9节点分支DAG，55个变量，1191条记录）数据集。

Result: GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6在CDISC-Bench上达到100%结构化匹配（49/49变量、254条正确记录），三次独立运行一致。最佳RAG基线仅达59.2%，所有单智能体和扁平多智能体方法均为0%。DAG拓扑的赋能效果显著：GPT-4.1在相同DAG下达到59.2%的均值结构匹配，而在其他所有架构下均为0%。方法在ADAE数据集推广中首次尝试就实现100%结构化匹配，验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将领域流程知识编码为图拓扑（而非依赖LLM推理能力本身）是实现可靠、符合GxP规范的临床试验编程的关键使能因素。DAG结构将复杂的监管流程分解为可验证、可重试的模块化节点，不仅能大幅提升顶尖模型的成功率，还能显著增强较弱模型的性能，展示了流程级知识工程在LLM应用中的核心价值。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [147] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Aegis，一个运行时治理系统，将模型输出视为"行动提案"，在工具执行前通过可信决策层进行中介。系统根据策略状态评估提案、在服务端解析来源、不确定性时默认失败关闭，并通过Senate式仲裁机制处理特定案例。在包含6,300行的沙盒语料库评测中，基线提示词策略条件下出现79行风险路径泄漏，而Aegis治理条件下实现零风险副作用完成和零风险工具应用，同时保留完整的来源与仲裁证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统请求的工具操作可能产生修改文件、发送消息、启动任务等有害操作副作用，将安全问题从有害文本生成转变为有害操作副作用。提示词级别的治理虽能塑造模型行为，但无法创建执行边界，因此需要一种可信的运行时治理机制来确保模型输出不会直接导致有害的操作后果。

Method: Aegis系统将模型输出视为行动提案，通过可信决策层进行中介：根据活动策略状态评估提案、在服务端解析来源（provenance）以确保可追溯性、在不确定性情况下默认失败关闭（fail closed）、并将选定案例路由至Senate式仲裁机制——一种基于法定人数（quorum）的非单边授权路径。评测在重复沙盒语料库上进行，涵盖五个运行族、42个任务、三种条件和每个族十次重复。

Result: 在6,300行数据中，纯提示词策略条件产生79行有风险比较器路径泄漏；在2,100行Aegis治理数据中，记录到零次治理模拟工具应用和零次治理风险副作用完成。所有1,832行Aegis尝试治理的行都保留了可信的Aegis解析来源，所有1,019行Senate仲裁行都有法定人数和最终签名票数证据。

Conclusion: 这些结果不证明通用自主代理安全性，但支持一个更窄的系统性主张：在该评测沙盒语料库中，运行时行动边界治理防止了观察到的风险提案成为治理副作用。这强调了"模型提议、可信运行时决策"这一架构范式的价值。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [148] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过注册配对对比实验，研究了API合同中"推理努力（reasoning-effort）"条款对成本和准确率的影响。以Sonnet 5为对象，使用30道AIME 2026题目、每题5次调用，对比显式高努力合同与省略该条款的合同。结果显示显式高努力合同平均每次调用成本高出$0.01031（95%CI：+\$0.00204~+\$0.01974），准确率差异未检测到（+0.0133，CI：-0.0267~+0.0467）。每个正确答案的成本在显式高努力合同下为$0.08665，省略条款合同下为$0.07662。作者还通过模型API元数据、预注册原始响应探针等记录了模型特定的省略语义，所有分析流程均在查看结果前冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API采购的是带日期的合同而非单纯的模型名称，合同中包含推理努力条款等多项内容。作者希望严谨地评估推理努力条款的显式声明（特别是显式高努力与省略该条款）对成本和准确率的影响，并强调预注册与审计级方法论以确保结果可信。

Method: 采用注册配对对比设计：对同一模型Sonnet 5，比较显式高努力合同与省略努力条款的合同。使用30道AIME 2026题目、每题5次调用。每次付费尝试分配一个冻结的终端类别，推理时对题目进行重采样但保留同一题的重复调用。统计计划、请求注册表、解析器、终端分类法及分析管道均在分析结果前冻结。辅以Models API元数据、预注册原始响应探针等文档化模型特定的省略语义。

Result: 成本方面：显式高努力合同平均每次调用成本比省略条款高出$0.01031（95%置信区间：+\$0.00204~+\$0.01974），即显式高努力显著更贵。准确率方面：对比为+0.0133，95%置信区间[-0.0267, +0.0467]，未检测到统计显著的准确率差异，但该区间不能排除最多4.67个百分点的增益。经济效率方面：每个正确答案的成本在显式高努力下为$0.08665，省略条款下为$0.07662，即省略条款的经济效率更优。

Conclusion: 显式高努力条款会显著提高API调用成本，但未检测到相应的准确率收益，因此在所研究的模型、任务与日期范围内，省略推理努力条款的经济效率更优。作者强调结论受限于模型（Sonnet 5）、任务（AIME 2026）和采集日期，且所有推断均基于预注册的冻结流程，以保证可复核性。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [149] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedPref是一种联邦偏好学习方法，利用冻结的公共语言模型生成候选JSON提取结果，由本地标注进行排序，并通过跨机构协作训练Qwen3-8B适配器来从放射报告中提取结构化关系，仅共享模型更新而不共享原始报告或标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本记录发现和位置，但下游检索与分析需要固定模式的关系抽取。不同医疗机构标注数据分布不均，小医院本地证据不足，而直接聚合数据可能不可行（涉及隐私或数据隔离限制）。

Method: FedPref使用冻结的公共语言模型作为异构教师池来生成多种候选JSON提取结果，本地标注对候选结果进行排序打分，各站点协作训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器，但仅共享模型参数更新。异构教师池通过跨模型对比避免了重复单模型采样导致的模式坍缩。

Result: 在六个模拟医院的开发数据上（数据量和疾病患病率不均），FedPref相比各站点独立训练，客户端平均F1提升2.49点，最差站点F1提升9.10点，且数据量最少的站点获益最大。中心化池化训练在客户端平均F1上高出2.66点。在锁定的400份人工校验金标准测试集上，FedPref达到68.68 F1，池化训练为71.67，保持相同排序。

Conclusion: FedPref使数据量不均且无法聚合数据的机构能够在无需共享报告或标注的前提下从协作中获益，验证了联邦偏好学习在分布式医学信息抽取中的有效性。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [150] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人机协作数学发现范式FAR（Find, Attempt, and Recommend），将人类从预先选定单一问题的工作模式转变为选定研究方向，由系统自动在文献库中搜索候选问题并经过多级过滤后推荐给专家审阅。在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中挖掘出4,717个仍开放的猜想，最终筛选出77项供作者团队审查，发现了多个重要结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助数学发现工作流中，人力集中在起始阶段（选择研究问题）和末尾阶段（审查产物），这两个阶段正成为研究级数学的瓶颈。由于前沿模型推理和专家评审都是稀缺资源，如何有效分配这些资源以提高AI辅助数学发现效率是核心动机。

Method: 提出FAR级联框架，分为三个阶段：1）Find：从大规模文献语料中搜索候选猜想/开放问题；2）Attempt：对候选问题进行自动推理尝试；3）Recommend：通过自动化分流（triage）筛选出最优潜力的结果推荐给专家团队审阅。该范式将人类输入从"单个预选问题"转变为"研究方向"。

Result: 在组合学试点中，FAR从5,245篇论文中恢复6,453个候选猜想或开放问题，过滤为4,717个表述良好且仍然开放的问题；经自动推理和分流后筛出598个潜在解答，最终选出77项供人工审查。发现多个重要结果，涉及Davies-Jenssen-Perkins-Roberts、Erdős-Straus、Ikenmeyer-Pak-Panova和Lund-Saraf-Wolf等人的猜想与问题。

Conclusion: FAR证明了文献到评审的级联模式能有效实现大规模自动化数学发现，通过将人力从问题选择阶段解放出来、集中于审阅已过滤的高质量结果，显著提升了AI辅助数学研究的效率，是一种有效的"研究方向驱动"的人机协作新范式。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [151] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEffect 是一个"检查后降级"（checked-lowering）运行时系统，用于在 Agent 工具调度时强制执行异构的内存约束。通过独立的检查器在授予执行权限前重建和审计每个提议的程序降级过程，利用可审计的有界实现技术，在六个算子族和六个插件中显著降低峰值内存并提高固定容量下的任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills 可以将工具使用的过程和资源义务规范化为具体程序，但即使语义正确的程序也可能会加载整个输入，超出单个工具调用可用的内存上限。需要一个机制来保证工具调用在给定内存边界内执行，而不仅仅是语义正确。

Method: SkillEffect 采用"检查后降级"架构：(1) 每个计算步骤需要可恢复的源关系、可审计的有界实现和注册的输出后置条件；(2) 独立的检查器在授予执行权限前，从提交的程序和不可变输入中重建每个提议的降级；(3) 每个关系插件提供源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造函数、arena边界函数和后置条件；(4) 共享的运行时提供检查后选择、有界VM执行、原子容量租借和分级发布机制。

Result: 六个算子族中有界访问显著降低了峰值内存并提高了外部固定容量下的完成率；六个插件在五种执行模式（从流式归约到有界堆Top-k）中实例化同一契约；XLSX入门研究和一个新的Top-k扩展表明新关系和新保留状态模式可复用同一信任边界；检查器接受所有合法的配置并拒绝所有对抗性提议。

Conclusion: 单一的检查后降级架构可以在 Agent 工具调度层面强制执行异构的、已注册的内存关系。SkillEffect 的通用性是架构层面而非自动化的——每个受支持的计算都需要审计过的关系插件，而调度、资源控制、执行和发布机制则是跨插件共享的。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [152] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"记忆-信号前沿"（remembering-signaling frontier）概念，用于描述在计算资源受限（记忆与通信均有预算）的智能体系统中，历史记忆与同伴通信之间的权衡关系。论文假设：当历史信息能带来更大的任务损失缩减时，有界智能体所需的同伴通信量将更少。初步指称博弈实验显示，目标重复能缩短成功消息长度，而隐藏循环规则的可预测性并未缩短消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界智能体在做决策时可以从自身历史记忆或同伴信号中获取信息，但两种资源都受到限制。在资源受限条件下，如何最优地在记忆与通信之间分配信息预算是一个关键问题。现有工作缺乏一个统一的框架来描述记忆与信号速率之间的权衡关系。

Method: 论文建立了一个理论框架：在固定任务与决策规则下，满足性能阈值的（记忆速率，消息速率）组合构成可实现区域，其有效边界被定义为"记忆-信号前沿"。通过初步指称博弈实验，比较了目标重复（真实历史相关性）与隐藏循环规则（预测性但非显式历史相关性）两种条件下消息长度变化，用以检验核心假设。

Result: 初步实验结果显示：在指称博弈中目标重复与更短的成功消息相关，而来自隐藏循环规则的可预测性并未缩短消息。这为"历史记忆提供更大损失缩减时，所需的同伴通信更少"这一假设提供了初步支持。

Conclusion: 论文提出了记忆-信号前沿作为分析有界智能体记忆与通信资源权衡的理论工具，并给出了初步实验证据支持"历史收益替代通信需求"的核心预测。未来工作可通过系统变化记忆与消息速率来估计前沿曲线，并在多种合作任务中验证该预测。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [153] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiSCO是一个零样本、纯黑盒的文本到图像生成模型安全防御模块，通过在提示词层面进行分布引导的后缀扩展（beam search），结合对比评分和迭代自适应反馈，有效降低NSFW内容生成的成功率（ASR），适用于任何模型且无需修改模型内部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型存在生成NSFW内容的安全隐患，尤其是在红队对抗攻击下问题加剧。现有防御方法大多依赖白盒假设（如文本编码器优化、权重编辑、推理时干预），无法扩展到专有模型；而黑盒替代方案（如基于LLM的提示词改写）在"良性对抗"（benign adversarial）问题上失效——即提示词在语言层面看似安全，但由于模型学到的数据分布仍会触发有害生成。

Method: DiSCO采用纯黑盒、零样本的提示词级防御方案：1) 通过beam search进行分布引导的suffix扩展；2) 使用目标模型自身生成的安全与不安全图像池进行对比评分（contrastive scoring）来优化；3) 通过迭代自适应反馈持续调整，直到生成安全内容。该方法作为即插即用模块，无需模型重训练、微调或访问模型内部信息。

Result: 在I2P基准测试上，DiSCO在多种红队攻击场景下，对未防御和已防御模型分别实现了37.7%和25.13%的攻击成功率（ASR）降低，同时保持了语义保真度并提升了图像一致性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为一种黑盒、架构无关的防御模块，可直接应用于任何文本到图像生成系统，无需对模型本身做任何改动，为解决黑盒场景下的NSFW内容防御提供了可行且有效的方案。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [154] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KernelArc是一个多智能体框架，用于在异构工作负载下自主优化GPU内核。该框架通过纯结论共享内存、确定性基准守护和只读跨智能体状态（配合平台触发式草稿）实现策略特化智能体的并行协作。在NVIDIA H100和B200上使用SOL-ExecBench工作进行评估，生成的实现覆盖自定义BF16 GEMM、cuBLASLt Expert配置表、融合MoE反向、形状门控解码器层融合、原生NVFP4分组查询注意力及分页预填充注意力。在2026年7月30日的SOL-ExecBench公开排行榜上，这些提交在代表性L1、L2、量化及FlashInfer任务中均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化通常依赖专家经验与手工调优，难以覆盖日益增长的异构工作负载。作者提出多智能体协作搜索框架，旨在固定候选预算内通过共享搜索拓展探索空间并达到更优的初始解（incumbent），同时验证不同协调特征在不同内核与优化阶段的价值。

Method: 提出KernelArc多智能体框架，核心设计包括：1) 策略特化智能体并行运行，各自专注于特定优化策略；2) 智能体间通过"结论-only"共享内存通信，避免信息冗余与干扰；3) 确定性基准守护确保评测可复现与公平；4) 只读跨智能体状态配合平台期触发的草稿机制（plateau-triggered drafting）以促进跨智能体知识迁移。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上，使用SOL-ExecBench类别代表性工作负载评估，成功产生覆盖六类任务的优化内核实现，包括自定义BF16 GEMM、cuBLASLt Expert-API配置表、融合MoE反向传播、形状门控解码器融合、原生NVFP4分组查询注意力及分页预填充注意力。在2026年7月30日的SOL-ExecBench公开排行榜快照中，这些提交在L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer代表性任务上均排名第一。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持论文的核心论点：共享的多智能体搜索能在固定候选预算内拓宽探索并达到更强的初始解。同时，各协调特征（如结论共享、只读状态、草稿机制）的价值因内核类型和优化阶段而各异，提示协调策略需要自适应设计。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [155] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection)方法，通过训练线性门控在答案token的隐藏状态上选择高分候选，并引入decodability指标来预测隐藏状态选择是否优于多数投票。实验表明CASE在中等难度问题提升19个点，难题提升16.8个点，decodability与准确率增益的Pearson相关为0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在困难问题上不可靠，因为采样答案间存在相关性错误，错误答案可能获胜；基于隐藏状态读取正确性信号的选择方法在不同模型和任务上精度不稳定，且缺乏可信度度量。需要一种动态选择组合器及可靠的指标来决定何时使用隐藏状态选择而非投票。

Method: 提出CASE方法：在答案token的隐藏状态上训练一个线性门控，对每个候选进行打分并选择最高分者。同时提出decodability度量——一种无泄漏的量度，衡量门控将正确候选排在错误候选之上的能力。研究发现传统探测方法的准确性源于question-identity泄漏，在question-grouped评估下会消失。

Result: 在held-out数据上，decodability预测选择相对于投票的准确率增益的Pearson相关系数为r=0.75，决策阈值接近AUC=0.60。在通用和医学LLM上，CASE在中等难度问题上比投票提升最多19个点，在难题上提升16.8个点。decodability预测可迁移到未见的科学领域，误差在3.8个点以内。

Conclusion: Decodability不依赖于模型规模，而是取决于模型需要回忆的对齐知识。它为给定模型和任务提供了一种可预先测量的实用准则，用于在learned selection和majority voting之间做出选择。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [156] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"协作观察"（cooperative observation）这一框架，指出个人AI的效能受观测瓶颈制约，而观测瓶颈具有协作结构——系统的有用性、用户的信任与未来数据访问权限之间存在反馈循环。作者以Organizm原型六个月的初步单用户案例为支撑，并给出评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI系统需要建立用户目标、约束和持续承诺的模型，但模型质量受限于系统可观测的信息。此外，更广泛的观测本身并不能自动提升辅助质量，因为有限系统必须对信息进行选择和压缩。作者认为这一观测瓶颈具有协作结构，值得作为个人智能的统一分析框架提出。

Method: （1）概念论证：基于观测瓶颈的协作结构，提出"协作观察"框架，描述系统构建用户部分模型、用户评估系统行为、用户同意与控制塑造未来观测权限的三方反馈循环。（2）实证：报告Organizm原型六个月的初步单主体（single-subject）使用记录。（3）评估展望：概述衡量观测质量如何影响个人AI表现的评估方向。

Result: 提出"协作观察"作为个人智能的框架性概念；初步单主体经验来自Organizm六个月使用，但仅作为初步报告；尚未给出量化实验结果，主要为概念框架与评估方向的设计性贡献。

Conclusion: 个人AI的辅助质量与观测通道相互依赖：有用且可检查的行为可促使用户维持或扩展观测权限，而失败则导致用户纠正、收窄、撤销乃至放弃观测。作者主张将这一反馈循环视为个人智能研究的基础框架。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [157] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KNOWSIM，一个基于知识状态显式建模的用户模拟器评估框架，用于评估大语言模型的信息校准能力。该框架通过信息单元图表示用户知识状态，并依据学习理论规则模拟知识演化，计算知识增益、传递校准度和认知过载三个指标。在705个人机对话会话中验证，KNOWSIM与人类判断有73-74%的一致性，优于三个基线模拟器；在9个LLM上的评估揭示了标准评估无法发现的"能力倾向-处理交互"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器不显式建模用户知识，既无法在不同知识水平下产生真实的交互，也无法反映知识演化过程中交互的动态变化，这限制了LLM在知识密集型任务中信息校准能力的评估与训练。

Method: 构建以信息单元为节点、带先决关系图的知识状态表示，依据学习理论驱动的更新规则模拟知识演化；基于知识状态轨迹计算知识增益、传递校准度和认知过载三个指标；在705个跨领域、跨知识水平的真实人机会话中验证，并与三个基线模拟器对比。

Result: KNOWSIM的排名与人类判断有73-74%的符号一致性，优于三个基线模拟器；在9个LLM上的评估发现最优模型随用户知识水平变化，揭示了标准评估中不可见的"能力倾向-处理交互"效应。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户知识状态的模拟器能为LLM信息校准能力提供更真实、更细粒度的评估，且能揭示标准评估框架无法捕捉的模型间差异。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity.
